## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

- Inheritance: three constructor/destructor vptr stores and RTTI facet offsets prove direct `NewGroupPane : PanelPane`; EventHandler and TimerHandler are inherited through Pane, not repeated direct bases.
- Complete size: `GeneralPurposePanel__Constructor` allocates exactly `0x110` before calling `0x0056ca20`; scalar deleting wrapper `0x00573570` uses the same complete-object size. This resolves the class-size blocker.
- Layout: PanelPane ends at `0xf8`; no NewGroupPane body accesses `+0xf8..+0xfb`. Because explicit unread object storage cannot safely be called compiler padding when reproducing class size, the H preserves it as `unsigned char m_unusedState[4]` and documents the unknown original role. The constructor's 16-bit zero at `+0xfc` initializes two signed byte fields together. `+0xfe..+0xff` are compiler alignment before the child pointer.
- Field names: use `m_firstVisibleMember`, `m_groupMemberCount`, `m_scrollPane`, `m_numericGlyphs`, `m_toggleButtonState`, and `m_expanded`. These are descriptive/inferred, consistent with accepted project vocabulary, and materially safer than raw offsets.
- `m_numericGlyphs`: constructor loads `L"9X11FONT.BIN"` with prefix/index `99`, DrawNumberString indexes it as 9x11 glyph data, and destructor frees it through MemoryMan. `char *m_numericGlyphs` matches current project style.
- `m_toggleButtonState`: dword at `+0x108` initializes to zero and ToggleExpandedMode writes `2` before relayout. Exact original enum spelling is stripped; the role-accurate int name is the best defensible source choice.
- `m_expanded`: byte at `+0x10c` selects 6 versus 12 rows and 367 versus 637 layout height, and ToggleExpandedMode flips it. `bool` is the best source type.
- `0x0056cbf0`: vtable slot `+0x0c`, direct base call to `0x004b8e20`, and child SetBounds call prove `UpdateRenderRegion`; `OnLayout` is rejected.
- `0x0056cc80`: vtable slot `+0x2c`, `retn 8` shape inherited from Pane::SetBounds, origin write at `+0xac`, localized rectangle, and redispatch through `+0x0c` prove `SetBounds(const RectBounds *, const RectBounds *)`. The unused second argument is retained in the declaration.
- Raw `0x0056e030`: unsigned row check `0..5`, fixed row rectangles, and `retn 8` prove file-local `GetGroupMemberRowRect(unsigned short, RectBounds *)`.
- Raw `0x0056e090`: loops rows `0..5`, uses the same geometry and PointInRect, returns row or `-1`, proving file-local `HitTestGroupMemberRow(int y, int x)` with signed short return.
- Raw `0x0056e570`: packet readers, bounded ANSI-to-wide conversion, member-id matching, HP/MP writes, map marker refresh, range update, invalidation, and `retn 4` prove member `UpdateGroupMemberStatus(const unsigned char *)`. `UpdateGroupEntry` is rejected as too broad because the body updates only status values.
- Raw `0x0056e820`: only part index 6 yields `(168,37,185,54)` before PointInRect and returns 6/-1, proving file-local `HitTestGroupToggleButton(int y, int x)`.
- No-route result: all four raw starts have zero xrefs; searches for VA, RVA, and raw-offset pointer forms return zero. That evidence classifies current liveness, not source authorship. Ordinary compiled bodies and in-TU inline duplication support retained source functions.
- Source placement: Group.cpp is preferred over a speculative new GroupPane.cpp because all current ownership/emitter roots, adjacent group variants, packet logic, custom scrollbar, and map-marker helpers converge there. The separate-file alternative remains historical possibility, not the current best route.
- Rejected alternatives: aggregate C++ emission, literal vtable arrays, direct EventHandler/TimerHandler bases, a `void *` glyph handle, unsigned member counts, raw `sub_` names, `OnLayout`, `OnOpen`/`OnClose`, and leaving raw helpers blank solely for absent xrefs.
- No encountered dependency is left as a broad "investigate later" task. Exact original lexical spellings remain inferential, but the Pane dependency questions are resolved and their deterministic source-facing decisions are applied to ordinary files. ### Pane Header Dependency Closure

The prior Pane formal was syntactically and semantically incomplete. C++ does not permit `Pane` to derive from forward-declared `GrafPort`, `EventHandler`, or `TimerHandler`; `LObject` was not declared at the `OnChangeMessage` use; `Point` and two by-value `Region` members were absent; and `RectBounds m_bounds` incorrectly placed a new rectangle at `+0xa8` even though the live layout proves `+0x44` is inherited `GrafPort::m_visibleBounds`. The exact support plan below resolves those defects instead of deferring them:

| Dependency | Current evidence | Deterministic source decision | Exact ordinary destinations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `RectBounds` / `Point` | UID00015S already emits `struct Point { int y; int x; }`; Pane embeds that exact eight-byte Y/X record at `+0xac`. | Move the one complete `Point` declaration into `RectBounds.h` immediately before `RectBounds`; remove only the duplicate declaration from UID00015S CPP. | `by-class/RectBounds.md`, `by-memory/0x004b7c30-0x004b7f87.RectGeometryHelpers.md`, `by-file/RectBounds.md`. |
| `Region` | Current UDT is exact `0x14`: `LObject` base/vptr plus `RectBounds m_bounds`; Pane embeds two at `+0xc4` and `+0xdc`; GrafPort embeds two at `+0x04` and `+0x54`. | Emit complete `Region.h`. Normalize the historical dirty-region `Motion::` spelling to `Region::` because the same bodies, receiver layout, constructors, callers, and Pane/GrafPort uses form one class contract; keep `Motion` as historical rejected documentation spelling, not a second layout-identical source class. | `by-class/Region.md`, UID0001FW, UID00042Q/42R/42S/42T, `by-file/Region.md`. |
| `GrafPort` | UID00005V/UID0000JR/UID0002PJ prove exact size `0xa0`, `LObject` inheritance, field layout through `+0x9c`, and the current method family. | Emit complete `GrafPort.h` with real by-value dependencies and natural alignment. `Pane.h` includes it; a forward declaration is rejected for inheritance. | `by-class/GrafPort.md`, `by-file/GrafPort.md`. |
| `EventHandler` | Exact four-byte polymorphic interface, vtable/default bodies, broad Pane consumers, and current docs' highest-probability shared-header direction. | Create `NexusTK/ui/core/EventHandler.h/.cpp`; move class owner/emitter to its validator-allocated by-file UID. `EventHandler.cpp` includes `EventDispatcher.h`; `EventDispatcher.h` includes `EventHandler.h`. The combined-header and Pane-owned alternatives are historicalized as lower probability. | new `by-file/EventHandler.md`, `by-class/EventHandler.md`, UID00014A, `by-file/EventDispatcher.md`, `by-class/EventDispatcher.md`. |
| `TimerHandler` | Exact four-byte polymorphic timer facet, destructor/wrappers, Pane tertiary view, and TimerMgr calls. | Create `NexusTK/util/TimerHandler.h/.cpp`; move class owner/emitter to its validator-allocated by-file UID. `TimerHandler.cpp` includes `TimerMgr.h`; `TimerMgr.h` includes `TimerHandler.h`. The historical folded-only TimerMgr declaration is rejected. | new `by-file/TimerHandler.md`, `by-class/TimerHandler.md`, UID0001K7, `by-class/TimerMgr.md`, `by-file/TimerMgr.md`. |
| `Pane` | UID0001VH fixes exact `0xf8` layout: bases end at `+0xa8`; fields occupy `+0xa8..+0xf6`; derived state starts `+0xf8`. | Emit complete base includes and the exact tail below. Use inherited `m_visibleBounds`, not false `m_bounds`. Names at `+0xb8/+0xbc/+0xc0` remain high-probability descriptive choices, but their sizes/roles are fixed and therefore do not justify omitting the layout. | `by-class/Pane.md`, `by-type/by-struct/PaneLayout.md`, `by-file/Pane.md`. |
| `PanelPane` / `NewGroupPane` | Fieldless `PanelPane` size `0xf8`; NewGroupPane size `0x110`; existing declarations already use the right direct inheritance. | Retain those declarations after the complete Pane chain lands. The emitting `NewGroupPane.h` declaration owns the applied replacement of same-directory `"PanelPane.h"` with `"../ui/core/PanelPane.h"`; `by-file/Group.md` records only the dependency/source-placement prose and does not emit that H edit. | `by-class/PanelPane.md`, `by-class/NewGroupPane.md`, `by-file/Group.md`. |

The source-tree decision follows current project conventions and period-appropriate coupling: small reusable interfaces receive their own headers; implementation files include the complete manager/router only where member calls require it; feature headers consume reusable bases rather than recreating local partial classes. No PCH-only or decompiler-local type assumption is accepted.

`LObject` does not need an illegal duplicate Pane-local declaration: the complete `GrafPort.h` payload includes `../util/LObject.h`, making both the direct GrafPort base and Pane's `OnChangeMessage(LObject *, Message *)` parameter legal at the Pane declaration point. EventHandler and TimerHandler are likewise complete included class definitions, not forward declarations used as bases.

Historical report-time collision/current-path checking found `by-file/EventHandler.md`, `by-file/TimerHandler.md`, and all six Pane child pages physically absent, with no active lease, B-agent goal, or unexecuted B report declaring their paths/ranges/source units. The callback repeated that check immediately before each creation, allocated UID0004ZN/UID0004ZO/UID0004ZP..UID0004ZU without collision, validated each page, and released every lease.

The exact object allocation at `0x004645ef` is `operator new(0x48)` immediately before the constructor call at `0x00464613`. The router at `0x004a7130` reads/writes `m_cursorHideArmed +0x34`, `m_cursorStillStartTick +0x38`, `m_cursorHideDelayMs +0x3c`, `m_lastCursorX +0x40`, and `m_lastCursorY +0x44`; the constructor initializes the first three and intentionally leaves the coordinate pair untouched. This proves a `0x48` object and rejects the older class/file prose that called `+0x44` the next-object boundary. Natural alignment supplies the three bytes after both boolean fields; only the handler record needs an explicit `reserved[3]` because UID00022A's accepted source bodies directly copy and clear those three bytes.

`EventDispatcher.h` is the one-definition owner for `EventHandlerRecord`, `EventHandlerRecordVector`, and `EventHandlerTree` because the class contains `EventHandlerTree` by value. UID00022A must therefore remove its three duplicate CPP-local declarations and include/consume this header while retaining `EventHandlerIterator`, `EventHandlerListNode`, and every helper body. UID0001UF records this declaration-versus-implementation ownership split. A PCH-only declaration, a forward-declared by-value tree, and duplicate CPP-local definitions are rejected as non-compilable or one-definition violations.

The existing accepted `TrackInputTarget(int,bool)` contract stores 32-bit target identities; Pane therefore uses the explicit period-correct 32-bit cast `reinterpret_cast<int>(this)` rather than changing the InputMan ABI.

The consumed `../util/List.h` must itself be real rather than a PCH fiction. Current [UID:000079] has the complete accepted declaration incorrectly in CPP and blank H. Its callback-ready CPP is `#include "List.h"`, a blank line, and `[[CHILDREN]]`; its H moves the existing declaration unchanged under `#include "LObject.h"`:

```cpp
#include "LObject.h"

typedef int (__cdecl *ListCompareFunction)(const void *left,
                                           const void *right);

class List : public LObject
{
public:
    List(int elementSize, int pageSize);
    virtual ~List();
    virtual void *GetData();
    virtual void *GetElementAt(int index);
    virtual void InsertAt(int index, int count, void *source);
    virtual void Append(int count, void *source);
    virtual void RemoveAt(int index, int count);
    virtual void RemoveAll();
    virtual void SwapElements(int firstIndex, int secondIndex);
    void AssignFrom(List *source);
    void SetCount(int count);
    int GetCount() const { return m_count; }
    void Sort(ListCompareFunction compare);

protected:
    int m_elementSize;
    int m_pageSize;
    int m_count;
    void *m_data;

private:
    void ReserveElementCapacity(int elementCount);
    void ReservePageCapacity(int pageCount);
    int PageCountForElementCount(int elementCount);
};

typedef char ListSizeMustBe20[sizeof(List) == 0x14 ? 1 : -1];

[[CHILDREN]]
```

#### Exact Pane H Disposition

The source-ready Pane H is:

```cpp
#ifndef NEXUSTK_UI_CORE_PANE_H
#define NEXUSTK_UI_CORE_PANE_H

#include "../../render/GrafPort.h"
#include "../../util/TimerHandler.h"
#include "EventHandler.h"
#include "RectBounds.h"
#include "Region.h"

class Layer;
class Message;
struct RuntimeClass;

class Pane : public GrafPort, public EventHandler, public TimerHandler
{
public:
    Pane();
    virtual ~Pane();
    virtual RuntimeClass *GetRuntimeClass();
    virtual void OnChangeMessage(LObject *owner, Message *message);
    virtual void UpdateRenderRegion(const RectBounds *bounds);
    virtual void DrawOnTarget(GrafPort *target, const RectBounds *bounds,
                              const RectBounds *clipBounds);
    virtual void Show();
    virtual void Hide();
    virtual Pane *GetParentPane();
    virtual void InvalidateRect(const RectBounds *bounds);
    virtual void GetDescription(int descriptionId, wchar_t *description);
    virtual RectBounds *GetScreenBounds(RectBounds *outBounds) const;
    virtual void SetBounds(const RectBounds *bounds,
                           const RectBounds *previousBounds);
    virtual void AddToLayer(RectBounds *bounds, int order,
                            Pane *previousPane, Layer *layer);
    virtual void InsertInLayer(RectBounds *bounds, int order,
                               Pane *previousPane, Layer *layer);
    virtual void RemoveFromLayer();
    virtual void SetPaneOrder(Pane *referencePane, bool placeAfter);
    virtual void UnregisterEventHandler();
    virtual void OnPaint();
    virtual bool HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleKeyOrTextEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleImeEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandlePacketEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleSystemOrControlEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleType19Event(Event *event);
    virtual void GetLocalOffset(Point *offset);
    virtual void GetScreenOffset(Point *offset);
    virtual bool ShouldAcceptEvent() const;
    virtual bool OnTimer(int timerId, int arg0, int arg1);

    void SetMode(unsigned char mode);
    bool IsAttachedToLayer() const;
    void SetFocusRegistration(bool active);

protected:
    Layer *m_layer;
    Point m_origin;
    bool m_visible;
    unsigned char m_mode;
    Pane *m_redrawPeerPane;
    unsigned int m_redrawState;
    bool m_redrawPending;
    Region m_pendingMotionRegion;
    bool m_inMotionPaint;
    Region m_motionRegion;
    unsigned int m_inputRegistrationState;
    bool m_pendingDelete;
    bool m_hasPendingMotionRegion;
    bool m_drawOnTarget;
};

typedef char PaneSizeMustBe248[sizeof(Pane) == 0xf8 ? 1 : -1];

#endif
```

This is the literal current formal H on disk. The dated command20406 callback payload lacked the include guard and ended in a header `[[CHILDREN]]` marker; subsequent no-loss support work preserved the complete declaration while converting it to the current conventional guarded form.

This layout is exact in size and offset under the established 32-bit MSVC ABI: bases occupy `0x00..0xa7`; `m_layer +0xa8`; `m_origin +0xac`; visibility/mode `+0xb4/+0xb5`; peer/state `+0xb8/+0xbc/+0xc0`; regions `+0xc4/+0xdc`; paint flag `+0xd8`; registration `+0xf0`; final booleans `+0xf4/+0xf5/+0xf6`; derived state begins `+0xf8`. The descriptive peer/state names are the highest-probability human names after rejecting layer, mode, deletion, and compiler-padding alternatives; their semantic uncertainty is documented but no longer used to omit required storage.

The exact Pane.cpp preamble is not the former self-header-only marker once `SetFocusRegistration` is emitted. It is:

```cpp
#include "Pane.h"
#include "../../input/InputMan.h"
#include "Layer.h"

```

#### Callback Validation Contract

For every existing dependency destination, acquire one short lease, apply only its ledger rows, run exactly `python .\tools\validator.py --mode file --file <exact-relative-path> --apply --queue-timeout 240 --no-generated-refresh`, record command ID/full timestamp/exit/ok/warnings/generated state, and release immediately. For each new by-file page, create it under lease, run the same scoped command to allocate its UID, substitute that literal UID into all dependent class/child owner-emitter metadata and report/manual payloads, validate those destinations serially, and release each lease. No `-coverage-report.md` or generated file is an agent edit.

## Positive Evidence Summary

- Read-only direct PE disassembly closes the Pane dependency island exactly. Starts `0x00544db0/dc0/dd0/de0/df0/e00` are six nine-byte source overrides ending in tail jumps to EventHandler defaults `0x004a8990/a0/b0/c0/d0/e0`; five internal seven-byte `0xcc` spans and trailing `[0x00544e09,0x00544e10)` padding are exact.

```cpp
void NewGroupPane::SetBounds(const RectBounds *bounds,
                             const RectBounds *previousBounds)
{
    RectBounds activeBounds;
    Point origin;
    (void)bounds;
    (void)previousBounds;

    InitPoint(&origin, activeBounds.left, activeBounds.top);
    m_origin = origin;
    OffsetRect(&activeBounds, -origin.x, -origin.y);
    UpdateRenderRegion(&activeBounds);
}
```

```cpp
void NewGroupPane::UpdateScrollRange()
{
    const int visibleRows = m_expanded ? 12 : 6;
```

```cpp
void NewGroupPane::OnPaint()
{
    EPFTileContext background;
    EPFTileContext rowFrame;
    EPFTileContext barFill;
    EPFTileContext barLeftCap;
    EPFTileContext barRightCap;
    EPFTileContext toggleButton;
    RectBounds rowBounds(6, 14, 161, 54);
    RectBounds sourceBounds;
    RectBounds destinationBounds;
    wchar_t percentText[128];

    DLPalette *inventoryPalette = g_pPaletteLib->GetPaletteByName(L"ITEMINV.PAL");
    g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(
        m_expanded ? L"ITEMINVE.EPF" : L"GROUP.EPF",
        m_expanded ? 3 : 0, &background);
    g_pfnBlitSprite(this, &background, &background.m_bounds,
                    &m_visibleBounds, 0, inventoryPalette, 0);

    SetDrawMode(1);
    SetTextBackColor(0);
    const int visibleRows = m_expanded ? 12 : 6;
    int firstMember = m_groupMemberCount - 6;
    if (m_firstVisibleMember + 6 <= m_groupMemberCount)
        firstMember = m_firstVisibleMember;
    if (firstMember < 0)
        firstMember = 0;
    const int lastMember = firstMember + visibleRows < m_groupMemberCount
        ? firstMember + visibleRows : m_groupMemberCount;
    if (m_expanded)
        rowBounds.Offset(5, 0);

    for (int memberIndex = firstMember;
         memberIndex < lastMember;
         ++memberIndex, rowBounds.Offset(0, 44))
    {
        UserPane::GroupMemberRecord &member =
            g_pUserPane->m_groupMembers[memberIndex];

        g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"GROUPCOL.EPF", 0, &rowFrame);
        RenderTileFrame(&rowFrame, &rowFrame.m_bounds, &rowBounds,
                        0, L"GROUPCOL.PAL", 0);

        HumanImageRenderParams appearance;
        appearance.Reset();
        appearance.faceId = member.faceId;
        appearance.hairId = member.hairId;
        appearance.faceColor = member.faceColor;
        appearance.hairColor = member.hairColor;
        appearance.skinColor = member.skinColor;
        appearance.bodyColor = member.bodyColor;
        appearance.helmetId = member.helmetId;
        appearance.helmetColor = member.helmetColor;
        appearance.faceDecorationId = member.faceDecorationId;
        appearance.faceDecorationColor = member.faceDecorationColor;
        appearance.hairDecorationId = member.hairDecorationId;
        appearance.hairDecorationColor = member.hairDecorationColor;
        appearance.secondFaceDecorationId = member.secondFaceDecorationId;
        appearance.secondFaceDecorationColor = member.secondFaceDecorationColor;

        g_pNewHumanImageLib->CalculateBounds(&appearance, &sourceBounds);
        destinationBounds = sourceBounds;
        destinationBounds.Offset(rowBounds.left + 4,
            rowBounds.top + (40 - sourceBounds.Height()) / 2);
        g_pNewHumanImageLib->Draw(this, &appearance,
                                  &sourceBounds, &destinationBounds);

        RectBounds nameBounds(rowBounds.left + 62, rowBounds.top + 6,
                              rowBounds.left + 162, rowBounds.top + 20);
        const unsigned char nameColor = member.active ? 0x0c : 0x8f;
        SetTextColor(nameColor);
        nameBounds.Offset(-1, -2);
        DrawTextInRect(member.name, wcslen(member.name), &nameBounds);
        nameBounds.Offset(1, 0);
        DrawTextInRect(member.name, wcslen(member.name), &nameBounds);
        SetTextColor(0x8f);

        if (member.currentHitPoints != 0)
        {
            g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"BAR.EPF", 0, &barFill);
            g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"BAR.EPF", 1, &barLeftCap);
            g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"BAR.EPF", 2, &barRightCap);

            RectBounds fullBar(rowBounds.left + 64, rowBounds.top + 20,
                               rowBounds.left + 149, rowBounds.top + 34);
            RectBounds fillBounds = fullBar;
            fillBounds.right = fullBar.right - fullBar.Width() / 2 - 4;
            if (member.maximumHitPoints != 0)
            {
                if (member.currentHitPoints < member.maximumHitPoints)
                {
                    fillBounds.left = fillBounds.right - MulDiv(
                        fillBounds.Width(), member.currentHitPoints,
                        member.maximumHitPoints);
                }

                sourceBounds = barFill.m_bounds;
                sourceBounds.left += fillBounds.left - fillBounds.right;
                RectBounds leftCapBounds = fillBounds;
                leftCapBounds.left = fillBounds.left - 3;
                leftCapBounds.right = fillBounds.left;
                RectBounds rightCapBounds = fillBounds;
                rightCapBounds.left = fillBounds.right;
                rightCapBounds.right = fillBounds.right + 3;
                RenderTileFrame(&barLeftCap, &barLeftCap.m_bounds,
                                &leftCapBounds, 1, L"BAR.PAL", 0);
                RenderTileFrame(&barFill, &sourceBounds, &fillBounds,
                                0, L"BAR.PAL", 0);
                RenderTileFrame(&barRightCap, &barRightCap.m_bounds,
                                &rightCapBounds, 1, L"BAR.PAL", 0);
            }
            else
            {
                fillBounds.left = fillBounds.right;
            }

            if (m_exposedRegion.IntersectsRect(&fillBounds))
            {
                fillBounds.Offset(-2, 3);
                swprintf_s(percentText, 128, L"%d%%",
                    static_cast<unsigned int>(
                        static_cast<double>(member.currentHitPoints) /
                        static_cast<double>(member.maximumHitPoints) * 100.0));
                fillBounds.left = fillBounds.right - 9 * wcslen(percentText);
                DrawNumberString(percentText, fillBounds.left,
                                 fillBounds.top, 2);
            }

            g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"BAR.EPF", 3, &barFill);
            g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"BAR.EPF", 4, &barLeftCap);
            g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"BAR.EPF", 5, &barRightCap);

            fillBounds = fullBar;
            fillBounds.left = fullBar.left + fullBar.Width() / 2 + 4;
            if (member.maximumManaPoints != 0)
            {
                if (member.currentManaPoints < member.maximumManaPoints)
                {
                    fillBounds.left = fillBounds.right - MulDiv(
                        fillBounds.Width(), member.currentManaPoints,
                        member.maximumManaPoints);
                }

                sourceBounds = barFill.m_bounds;
                sourceBounds.left += fillBounds.left - fillBounds.right;
                RectBounds leftCapBounds = fillBounds;
                leftCapBounds.left = fillBounds.left - 3;
                leftCapBounds.right = fillBounds.left;
                RectBounds rightCapBounds = fillBounds;
                rightCapBounds.left = fillBounds.right;
                rightCapBounds.right = fillBounds.right + 3;
                RenderTileFrame(&barLeftCap, &barLeftCap.m_bounds,
                                &leftCapBounds, 1, L"BAR.PAL", 0);
                RenderTileFrame(&barFill, &sourceBounds, &fillBounds,
                                0, L"BAR.PAL", 0);
                RenderTileFrame(&barRightCap, &barRightCap.m_bounds,
                                &rightCapBounds, 1, L"BAR.PAL", 0);
            }
            else
            {
                fillBounds.left = fillBounds.right;
            }

            if (m_exposedRegion.IntersectsRect(&fillBounds))
            {
                fillBounds.Offset(-2, 3);
                swprintf_s(percentText, 128, L"%d%%",
                    static_cast<unsigned int>(
                        static_cast<double>(member.currentManaPoints) /
                        static_cast<double>(member.maximumManaPoints) * 100.0));
                fillBounds.left = fillBounds.right - 9 * wcslen(percentText);
                DrawNumberString(percentText, fillBounds.left,
                                 fillBounds.top, 2);
            }
        }
    }

    g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"INVENBUT.EPF",
        7 * m_toggleButtonState + (m_expanded ? 3 : 4), &toggleButton);
    RectBounds toggleBounds(168, 37, 185, 54);
    if (m_expanded)
        toggleBounds.Offset(5, 0);
    RenderTileFrame(&toggleButton, &toggleButton.m_bounds, &toggleBounds,
                    0, L"invenbut.pal", 0);
}
```

```cpp
bool NewGroupPane::HandleKeyOrTextEvent(Event *event)
{
    const unsigned char key = g_pEventMan->TranslateEventKey(event->key,
                                                              event->modifier);
    if (event->type != 8) return false;

    if (key == 0x93 && event->modifier == 0) {
        m_firstVisibleMember -= 6;
        if (m_firstVisibleMember < 0) m_firstVisibleMember = 0;
        UpdateScrollRange();
    } else if (key == 0x94 && event->modifier == 0) {
        m_firstVisibleMember += 6;
        if (m_firstVisibleMember >= m_groupMemberCount - 6) {
            m_firstVisibleMember = m_groupMemberCount - 6;
        }
        UpdateScrollRange();
    } else if (key == 'f' && event->modifier != 2) {
        ToggleExpandedMode();
        m_toggleButtonState = 0;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    InvalidateRect(&m_visibleBounds);
    return true;
}
```

```cpp
bool NewGroupPane::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *event)
{
    if (g_pUserPane->m_inputLocked) return false;

    if (event->type == 0) {
        if (!PointInRect(event->y, event->x, &m_visibleBounds)) {
            m_toggleButtonState = 0;
            return false;
        }
        RectBounds toggleBounds;
        GetGroupToggleButtonRect(6, &toggleBounds);
        m_toggleButtonState = PointInRect(event->y, event->x, &toggleBounds) ? 1 : 0;
        InvalidateRect(&m_visibleBounds);
        return true;
    }

    if (event->type == 1 && HitTestGroupToggleButton(event->y, event->x) == 6) {
        ToggleExpandedMode();
        m_toggleButtonState = 2;
        InvalidateRect(&m_visibleBounds);
    } else if (event->type == 7 &&
               PointInRect(event->y, event->x, &m_visibleBounds)) {
        short position = static_cast<short>(m_scrollPane->m_scrollPosition -
                                            event->wheelDelta);
        if (position < 0) position = 0;
        if (position > m_scrollPane->m_scrollRange) {
            position = m_scrollPane->m_scrollRange;
        }
        if (position != m_scrollPane->m_scrollPosition) {
            m_firstVisibleMember = static_cast<signed char>(position);
            m_scrollPane->SetScrollPosition(position);
            UpdateScrollRange();
            InvalidateRect(&m_visibleBounds);
        }
    }

    MapRect scrollMap;
    m_scrollPane->GetMapRect(&scrollMap);
    event->x -= scrollMap.top;
    event->y -= scrollMap.left;
    const bool handled = m_scrollPane->HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(event);
    event->x += scrollMap.top;
    event->y += scrollMap.left;
    return handled;
}
```

```cpp
bool NewGroupPane::HandlePacketEvent(Event *event)
{
    const unsigned char *packet = event->packetData;
    if (packet[0] != 99) return false;

    switch (packet[1]) {
    case 2:
        return ParseGroupListPacket(packet + 2);
    case 3:
        return UpdateGroupMemberStatus(packet + 2);
    default:
        return false;
    }
}
```

```cpp
bool NewGroupPane::ParseGroupListPacket(const unsigned char *packetData)
{
    const unsigned char *read = packetData;
    const unsigned char memberCount = *read++;

    if (m_groupMemberCount > memberCount) {
        for (int i = 0; i < m_groupMemberCount; ++i) {
            g_activeMapPane->RemoveGroupMemberHitBar(
                g_pUserPane->m_groupMembers[i].memberId);
        }
    }

    g_pUserPane->m_groupMemberCount = memberCount;
    m_groupMemberCount = memberCount;

    for (int i = 0; i < memberCount; ++i) {
        GroupMemberRecord &member = g_pUserPane->m_groupMembers[i];
        member.memberId = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(read); read += 4;

        const unsigned int nameLength = *read++;
        char nameBuffer[128];
        memcpy(nameBuffer, read, nameLength);
        read += nameLength;
        nameBuffer[nameLength] = 0;
        const int wideLength = MultiByteToWideChar(0, 0, nameBuffer,
                                                   nameLength, member.name, 128);
        member.name[wideLength] = 0;

        member.active = (*read++ != 0);
        member.faceId = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(read); read += 2;
        member.hairId = *read++;
        member.hairColor = *read++;
        member.faceColor = *read++;
        member.skinColor = *read++;
        member.bodyColor = *read++;
        member.helmetId = *read++;
        member.helmetColor = *read++;
        member.faceDecorationId = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(read); read += 2;
        member.faceDecorationColor = *read++;
        member.hairDecorationId = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(read); read += 2;
        member.hairDecorationColor = *read++;
        member.secondFaceDecorationId = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(read); read += 2;
        member.secondFaceDecorationColor = *read++;
        member.maximumHitPoints = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(read); read += 4;
        member.currentHitPoints = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(read); read += 4;
        member.maximumManaPoints = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(read); read += 4;
        member.currentManaPoints = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(read); read += 4;
        g_activeMapPane->RefreshGroupMemberHitBar(member.memberId);
    }

    UpdateScrollRange();
    InvalidateRect(&m_visibleBounds);
    return true;
}
```

- Reachability/evidence: two OnPaint calls, exact resource/palette lookups, glyph buffer at +0x104. No separate font-module owner exists.

```cpp
void NewGroupPane::DrawNumberString(const wchar_t *text,
                                    int left,
                                    int top,
                                    unsigned char colorVariant)
{
    EPFTileContext glyph;
    EPFTileContext punctuation;
    RectBounds destination;
    EPFTileContext_Initialize(&glyph);
    EPFTileContext_Initialize(&punctuation);
    glyph.rowStridePixels = 9;
    InitRectBounds(&glyph.bounds, 0, 0, 9, 11);
    InitRectBounds(&destination, left, top, left + 9, top + 11);

    while (*text != 0) {
        int punctuationFrame = -1;
        if (*text == L'/') punctuationFrame = 0;
        else if (*text == L'%') punctuationFrame = 1;
        else if (*text == L'(') punctuationFrame = 2;
        else if (*text == L')') punctuationFrame = 3;

        if (punctuationFrame >= 0) {
            g_pEPFLib->LookupLayoutEntry(L"COMMA.EPF", punctuationFrame,
                                         &punctuation);
            RenderTileFrame(&punctuation, &punctuation.bounds,
                            &destination, true, L"COMMA.PAL", 0);
        } else if (*text != L' ') {
            glyph.pixelData = m_numericGlyphs +
                99 * (*text + 10 * colorVariant - L'/');
            RenderTileFrame(&glyph, &glyph.bounds, &destination,
                            true, NULL, 0);
        }
        ++text;
        OffsetRect(&destination, 9, 0);
    }
}
```

```cpp
void NewGroupPane::ToggleExpandedMode()
{
    RectBounds bounds;
    m_toggleButtonState = 2;
    m_expanded = !m_expanded;
    RemoveFromLayer();
    GetBounds(&bounds);
    SetBounds(&bounds, NULL);
    AddToLayer(&bounds, 0, g_pBackPane, g_activeLayer);
    UpdateScrollRange();
    InvalidateRect(&m_visibleBounds);
}
```

```cpp
void Pane::SetFocusRegistration(bool active)
{
    int state = active ? 1 : 0;

    if (m_inputRegistrationState == state)
        return;

    m_inputRegistrationState = state;
    g_pInputMan->TrackInputTarget(reinterpret_cast<int>(this), active);
}
```

It preserves the five corrected primary declarations and is the exact current formal H rather than the dated command20406 unguarded/header-child-marker form:

```cpp
#ifndef NEXUSTK_UI_CORE_PANE_H
#define NEXUSTK_UI_CORE_PANE_H

#include "../../render/GrafPort.h"
#include "../../util/TimerHandler.h"
#include "EventHandler.h"
#include "RectBounds.h"
#include "Region.h"

class Layer;
class Message;
struct RuntimeClass;

class Pane : public GrafPort, public EventHandler, public TimerHandler
{
public:
    Pane();
    virtual ~Pane();
    virtual RuntimeClass *GetRuntimeClass();
    virtual void OnChangeMessage(LObject *owner, Message *message);
    virtual void UpdateRenderRegion(const RectBounds *bounds);
    virtual void DrawOnTarget(GrafPort *target, const RectBounds *bounds,
                              const RectBounds *clipBounds);
    virtual void Show();
    virtual void Hide();
    virtual Pane *GetParentPane();
    virtual void InvalidateRect(const RectBounds *bounds);
    virtual void GetDescription(int descriptionId, wchar_t *description);
    virtual RectBounds *GetScreenBounds(RectBounds *outBounds) const;
    virtual void SetBounds(const RectBounds *bounds,
                           const RectBounds *previousBounds);
    virtual void AddToLayer(RectBounds *bounds, int order,
                            Pane *previousPane, Layer *layer);
    virtual void InsertInLayer(RectBounds *bounds, int order,
                               Pane *previousPane, Layer *layer);
    virtual void RemoveFromLayer();
    virtual void SetPaneOrder(Pane *referencePane, bool placeAfter);
    virtual void UnregisterEventHandler();
    virtual void OnPaint();
    virtual bool HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleKeyOrTextEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleImeEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandlePacketEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleSystemOrControlEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleType19Event(Event *event);
    virtual void GetLocalOffset(Point *offset);
    virtual void GetScreenOffset(Point *offset);
    virtual bool ShouldAcceptEvent() const;
    virtual bool OnTimer(int timerId, int arg0, int arg1);

    void SetMode(unsigned char mode);
    bool IsAttachedToLayer() const;
    void SetFocusRegistration(bool active);

protected:
    Layer *m_layer;
    Point m_origin;
    bool m_visible;
    unsigned char m_mode;
    Pane *m_redrawPeerPane;
    unsigned int m_redrawState;
    bool m_redrawPending;
    Region m_pendingMotionRegion;
    bool m_inMotionPaint;
    Region m_motionRegion;
    unsigned int m_inputRegistrationState;
    bool m_pendingDelete;
    bool m_hasPendingMotionRegion;
    bool m_drawOnTarget;
};

typedef char PaneSizeMustBe248[sizeof(Pane) == 0xf8 ? 1 : -1];

#endif
```
